Original prompt: The performance visibility menu is blocking some of the screen, make the Claims Division language more farm-like, and add more office detail.

## 2026-07-25 - Core production checklist: release hardening

- Added recurring physical claims-floor evidence without adding route blockers: claimant correspondence packets, redacted evidence strips, a claim-denial stamp, returned-appeal archive boxes, and a live claim-closure board.
- Verified the new File dossier in a live career: claimant identity, incident, requested remedy, delay cost, exact $1.20/$0/$0.60 paths, beneficiary/burden disclosure, and an accepted Coverage Exception receipt all render inside the compact office view.
- Verified responsive play at 390x844 portrait, 844x390 landscape, and 1280x720 desktop. The wrapper has no horizontal overflow, keeps a contained 16:9 canvas, and exposes 40px mobile action controls.
- Fixed the Node production wrapper so `/game/index.pck` and the other Godot payload files are served from `dist/client/game` instead of returning 404. Added a production-server test that verifies the rendered shell and byte/hash parity of the served PCK.
- Reframed Senior Roost as an optional post-campaign management file. The five-shift probation file and its restructuring outcome remain the authored ending; the visible wrapper no longer presents endless scaling as the core objective.
- Added `docs/CORE_CHECKLIST.md`, mapping every production-checklist concept to implementation and repeatable evidence, while keeping real-device and GPU sign-off explicit as external release work.

## 2026-07-25 - Core production checklist: claimant paths and lane identity

- Added stable claimant profiles to every Nest Damage, Predator Loss, and Appeals file. The selected-hen File tab now states who claimed, what happened, what remedy they need, and what a slow result costs them.
- Added one irreversible pre-55% claimant-path intervention per active file: Humane Settlement, Fast Denial, or Coverage Exception. Each path discloses exact Feed Fund cost, beneficiary, pace, shell-risk, and burden before selection.
- Made the satire causal: a clean Fast Denial improves immediate bureau pace at no filing cost, applies worker/audit pressure, and returns the same named claimant to the next shift's Appeals tray.
- Added distinct filed-path lane identities beyond color/value/deadline/risk: Nest repair relief restores handler morale, Predator Loss adds trauma stress, and Appeals changes audit order.
- Persisted claimant identity, resolution choice, lock, normalized cost, source file, and delayed appeal state. Older claim saves derive neutral standard handling; tampered resolution costs are normalized from stable path terms.
- Added `claimant_resolution_test.gd` plus claimant-path and lane-disclosure assertions in the live routing UI test. Focused claimant, routing, simulation persistence, campaign save, and contract persistence tests pass.
- Raised the isolated Godot test ceiling from 90 to 150 seconds after the complete First Clutch induction path proved correct but required 117-132 seconds on this workstation. The affected shard now reports 63/63; combined with the other passing shards, the pre-feature suite was 188/188.

## Management gameplay loop pass

- Added an always-visible `ShiftQuotaProgress` objective and `CLEAN CLUTCH` streak readout to the top HUD. Sound and golden eggs grow the streak, award a capped per-egg Feed Fund bonus, and trigger audiovisual feedback; cracked eggs reset it.
- Changed the opening flow to begin paused with contextual `START HERE` guidance so the player can understand the target, inspect the flock, and deliberately start at 1×, 3×, or 10×.
- Added three permanent, five-level Coop Requisitions with rising costs and authoritative simulation effects:
  - **Beak-Friendly Keycaps:** +8% peckwork speed per level.
  - **Shell Integrity Lamp:** -2.5 percentage points of crack risk per level.
  - **Ergonomic Nest Pad:** -10% stress and fatigue gain per level.
- Connected every requisition to physical office feedback at all six workstations: purchased keycap levels appear on the keyboard, QA lamps appear and brighten, and nest pads appear and change color as comfort investment rises.
- Made the Feed Party a guarded once-per-shift event. Repeat purchases are denied without charging the fund, the button communicates availability, and the simulation pauses during synchronized trough attendance before restoring the prior speed.
- Clarified the overtime decision in the interface and simulation: +22% throughput is exchanged for sharply higher fatigue, stress, morale loss, and crack risk, and overtime resets automatically for the next shift.
- Added a modal farmer workday review that pauses the next shift, reports quota performance and egg quality, itemizes quota and quality bonuses plus feed cost, applies a bounded next-quota adjustment, and offers either immediate requisitions or the next shift.
- Added `OfficeAudioFeedback`, an eight-voice pooled procedural SFX system with reusable cues for sound, cracked, and golden eggs, streak pitch lift, approved requisitions, Feed Parties, UI input, and the farmer's review stamp.

## Peckwork routing and physical-credit pass

- Added focused routing and grading captures plus `claim_routing_test.gd`, `claim_routing_ui_test.gd`, and `egg_grading_feedback_test.gd`. The complete Godot 4.7 suite passes 17/17; Web export, frontend tests, lint, and production build pass under Node 22.

## Persistent hen careers and relationships

- Added deterministic career profiles for all six hens: Credit Conscious, Advancement Minded, and Quota Conditioned. Every worker now persists career XP, title, manager trust, grievance, and her last personnel action.
- Added four visible career tiers from Junior Claims Hen through Principal Shell Adjuster. Completed work awards quality-weighted XP; promotions improve throughput and shell quality while higher titles also carry a small strain burden.
- Added one flock-wide personnel check-in per shift through the selected-hen dossier: share basket credit, fund perch-side coaching, or authorize a stretch clutch. Profile matches are visible and improve the corresponding outcome.
- Made all three choices causal: credit improves the relationship but costs Feed Fund and farmer favor; coaching exchanges current speed for XP and safer shells; quota pressure produces an immediate speed/favor gain while creating lasting grievance, strain, and crack risk.
- Existing policy choices, incident responses, Feed Parties, and worked overtime now alter trust and grievances, so the flock remembers the broader management style instead of only the explicit check-in.
- Expanded the dossier and farmer review with career, profile, trust, grievance, check-in state, and the filed personnel outcome without increasing the dossier's 112-pixel footprint.
- Bumped the nested simulation checkpoint to v2. Legacy v1 saves migrate deterministically, and mid-shift saves preserve the global one-action guard and exact individual career state.
- Full Godot 4.7 regression suite passes 24/24, including deterministic profile/career causality, atomic one-per-shift personnel actions, v1-to-v2 migration, exact persistence, authoritative dossier routing, and the two-shift campaign integration path.
- Godot Web release export, the Node 22 Vinext production build, 2/2 rendered-wrapper tests, and lint all pass without warnings or errors.
- Live in-app WebGL verification completed from New Campaign through policy authorization and incident handling into a selected-hen dossier. Filing Mabel's profile-matched Share Credit check-in visibly raised XP and trust, recorded `FILED / MABEL`, and disabled the remaining personnel actions; the browser console is clean and the game is left paused on that result.

## Farm Mutual Contract Board and outside-peckwork economy

- Added the Day-3 Farm Mutual planning gate with three deterministic next-shift books. Homestead Stability reserves five slots at 18-file capacity and pays $10 or breaches for $5; Predator Watch reserves six at 24 capacity and pays $16 or breaches for $8; Exceptions Retention reserves six at 30 capacity and pays $24 or breaches for $12. Every folder's lane, scheduled arrival, rush flag, deadline, and clean/on-time target is disclosed before signing.
- Made signing transactional and reserve-safe. The complete folder count and authored breach clause must fit before acceptance; signing does not debit the Feed Fund, repeat signing cannot duplicate work, and an explicit `D` standard-book receipt prevents a planning deadlock when management declines outside work.
- Contract folders arrive at their authored times during the next live shift without opening a modal or stopping the clock. Queue and active-file surfaces identify `MUTUAL BINDER` and `CONTRACT RUSH` work with its deadline. Cracked or late folders cannot satisfy the binder even if completed.
- Settled the contract exactly once in the farmer review. Fulfillment premium and breach indemnity have their own ledger line, base flock production excludes the premium, net operating result includes the settlement once, and the closing Feed Fund remains authoritative.
- Advanced simulation persistence to schema v12. Review, explicit decline, running schedules, reserved IDs, completed/late quality sets, and settled results round-trip strictly; schema-v11 saves migrate to a neutral contract state, while malformed schedules, receipts, outcomes, and cross-ledger claim IDs fail atomically.
- Built a snapshot-driven left-wall physical fixture with wood frame, enamel header, three client folders, lane tokens, rush tabs, premium coins, binding clips, a lock shutter, active summary, and fulfillment/breach stamps. All 23 attached text layers now sit exactly 8 mm off their real host faces; the board is a secondary department landmark and adds no collision or navigation geometry.
- Added a responsive scrollable planning surface with full terms, `1`-`3` folder selection, exact Enter-to-sign intent, explicit decline, and receipt-gated `C` continuation. The campaign, probation, Senior, checkpoint, and accessible web status flows all use the same authoritative contract snapshot.

## Farm Mutual standing and Service Coop capital loop

- Added persistent client standing derived from settled work: each fulfilled binder contributes two points, each breached binder removes one, and the displayed total never falls below zero. Unlisted, Bronze, Silver, and Gold ranks unlock at 0 / 2 / 6 / 12 points; clean fulfillment streaks and the best streak are preserved, while a breach resets the current streak and places only that binder on a one-planning-day cooldown.
- Added the three-level `farm_mutual_service_coop` expansion. Bronze requires standing 2, 24 live-file capacity, and four active hens; Silver requires 6 / 30 / 5; Gold requires 12 / 36 / 6. Capital costs are $75 / $120 / $180 and total daily upkeep rises to $3 / $6 / $9.
- Made accreditation a real contract economy. Each installed tier adds 50% of the authored base premium on successful Farm Mutual binders only. The signing receipt freezes base value, installed tier, Service Coop bonus, and total, so later construction cannot rewrite an active agreement. Breaches never receive the bonus, and purchased tiers never demote when standing falls.
- Built a cumulative northeast-parcel facility with a Bronze seal counter and press, Silver dispatch tubes and courier hardware, and a Gold accreditation arch, standing totem, settlement vault, success light, and breach shutter. Its authoritative readouts, packets, seals, and settlement tray react to simulation snapshots without adding collision or navigation geometry.

## Farm Treasury and Department schema v23

- Advanced DepartmentSimulation to strict schema v23 with a conserving `farm_treasury_state`. Neutral v22 migration preserves only the real Feed Fund and completed-day chronology, inventing no principal, vendor/interest arrears, credit rating, or history; strict restore validates exact keys, integer cents, receipt chronology, deterministic replay, and one-cent tamper rejection before atomic commit.

Every accepted close proves `opening cash + inflow + credit draw = interest + vendors + labor + principal repaid + closing cash` exactly.
- Preserved labor as an external protected obligation: cash may pay wages only after interest/vendors, automatic credit can pay interest and vendor invoices but never labor, and any shortfall remains explicit wage arrears. Filed Treasury liabilities join protected reserve; debt with exhausted headroom forces spendable capital to zero instead of allowing an unfunded requisition.
- Added the standing/rating line economy: $50 base plus $5 per Farm Mutual standing point to a $150 pre-rating cap; three profitable debt-free closes advance Field File -> Steady Ledger -> Prime Roost; each rating adds $25 capacity and reduces the 5% per-shift rate by one point, to a $200/3% ceiling. Flockwatch exposes rating, line, principal, headroom, rate, vendors, interest, wages, and capital freeze; Farmer Review itemizes the complete conservation receipt.
- Focused `farm_treasury_state_test.gd`, `farm_treasury_department_test.gd`, `staffing_economy_test.gd`, simulation persistence, Farmgate, market, operations, petitions, feed, facilities, campus, and adjacent migration checks pass with exact zero-cash vendor draw, credit-never-wages, conservation, round-trip, and freeze coverage. Success returns the exact reserved stake and files seals; failure permanently spends the stake and awards none. Active stakes are excluded from available Roost Marks, preventing the same marks from also funding Career Sponsorship.

- Documented the player-facing route through the new systems: Flockwatch (`V`) for Treasury liabilities and live Senior/Board forecasts; the existing `1`-`3` plus `Enter` decision flow for annual mandate selection; Capital Blueprint -> Campus Portfolio for deeds/modules/staffing; and Continue/Return/`Esc` for receipt acknowledgement. Historical schema statements remain historical rather than being relabeled as current behavior.

## Environmental signage integration polish

- Audited the current office at gameplay scale and through the four authored 2560x1440 signage captures. Physical attachment was already sound; the remaining mismatch was visual hierarchy: a large back-wall fascia carried undersized lettering, modeled headings mixed with flat subtitles, and pale cubicle inserts became blank rectangles when overview LOD hid their microcopy.
- Enlarged the architectural title's authored face-height, converted architectural and destination subtitles to shallow modeled lettering with hidden semantic proxies, and retained the existing focus-aware LOD so subordinate copy only appears near the inspected location.
- Reworked cubicle nameplates into dark institutional laminate tags with brass memo rails, a registration tab, and a permanent egg seal. Names remain readable in close focus, while overview now retains purposeful office hardware instead of a pale empty card.
- Added regression contracts for neutral readable-face transforms, fully modeled title/subtitle treatment, stronger primary hierarchy, and non-text desk identity cues. `environmental_signage_hierarchy_test.gd`, `office_signage_test.gd`, and `environmental_text_fit_test.gd` pass after the change.
- Fresh `signage_back.png`, `signage_left.png`, `signage_desk.png`, and `signage_intake.png` captures were inspected at original 2560x1440 resolution. The back-wall bureau wordmark now has a decisive architectural scale, the modeled subtitle remains subordinate, and the desk insert reads as mounted dark laminate rather than a blank UI card.
- Fresh Web releases are byte-identical across all nine deployable payloads in `docs/` and `web/public/game/`. The 5,711,904-byte PCK has SHA-256 `47AF795CD81A2B67DBE8EC64B47569318E9DB43724949123F2271DADE7D01975`.
- The installed independent browser client produced a fresh canvas screenshot plus canonical diagnostic state with no error artifact at `output/web-game/environment-signage-final-client/`. The in-app browser also advanced through orientation and policy selection into the live office with zero warnings or errors.
- Responsive audits at 2560x1600, 1440x900, 390x844, and 844x390 report a loaded 16:9 Godot canvas, zero horizontal overflow, and zero console or page errors at `output/web-game/environment-signage-responsive-final/audit.json`; all four full-page captures were inspected at original resolution. `presentation_smoke_test.gd` and `office_detail_test.gd` pass alongside the three focused signage regressions.

## Opening experience, organic expansion, and menu audit

- Read-only audit confirms that the detailed 24 m x 18 m core office is worth preserving; the opening sprawl comes from a campus-sized base camera frame, unconditional construction of future facility roots and long circulation spines, Day-1 North Meadow presentation, and locked/empty parcels appearing before they matter.
- The first-clutch view simultaneously exposes the two-row HUD, routing queue, campaign badge, Flockwatch, coach card, selected-hen dossier, route/personnel controls, and ticker. Flockwatch then nests campaign, flock, staffing, Treasury, capital, facilities, applicants, Procurement, Farmgate, labor, directives, and metrics in one narrow scroll.
- Recommended a presentation-only reveal layer with `hidden`, `teased`, `offered`, and `commissioned` states derived from existing authoritative campaign/simulation data. Economy rules, costs, facility IDs, world coordinates, receipts, saves, and shortcuts remain unchanged.
- Recommended a core-office Day-1 camera, visible bounds derived only from discovered/owned content, one future expansion teaser at a time, segment-by-segment circulation growth, and reuse of the existing commissioning reveal for earned physical expansion.
- Recommended a minimal live HUD, one contextual hen drawer, progressive First Clutch controls, and domain-based Flockwatch pages for Today, Flock, Operations, Capital, and later Governance/Records. Full Blueprint, Portfolio, Settings, and review surfaces remain available as focused planning views.
- Proposed verification gates: at least 65-70% unobstructed world at 1280x720 during the first five minutes, no undiscovered parcel geometry in the Day-1 overview, one primary call to action, every current feature reachable once relevant, old-save restoration, unchanged economy receipts, safe keyboard/controller focus, and high-resolution captures at fresh, Day-3, Day-6, Day-9, and Senior milestones.
- No game or frontend source files were changed during this audit.

## Organic opening implementation integrated

- The office now derives presentation bounds from the occupied core plus only discovered, offered, pinned, or commissioned campus footprints. Future facility roots, distant meadow/portfolio geometry, unused corridor bays, and service trunks stay hidden until they become relevant; later saves reconstruct their earned campus without changing simulation data or coordinates.
- Flockwatch now preserves every existing control and signal across five independent pages: Today, Flock, Operations, Capital, and Governance / Records. First Clutch opens with Today and Flock, authored deep links discover the single relevant page, and `All Filings` remains a permanent escape hatch to the complete feature set.
- Roost Staffing content is separated into Flock, Operations, Capital, and Records presentation roots while retaining one authoritative component. Requisitions, Procurement, Farmgate, facilities, Treasury, applications, care, and governance actions continue to forward through their original signals.
- First Clutch now reveals the hen dossier and controls stage by stage: identity, routing, check-in, Priority Peck, then delivery. Advanced career, care, trust, and grievance detail is available through a compact details control, and skipping or completing induction restores the full management surface.
- Campaign, decision, review, commissioning, settings, and portfolio surfaces now suppress background HUD and routing chrome while active. Flockwatch likewise becomes the focused management surface instead of stacking over the live dossier.
- Focused component coverage passes for progressive campus reveal, Flockwatch page discovery and control reparenting, contextual First Clutch disclosure, Ready-first Blueprint filtering, and paged staffing with next-workstation-only signposting. Downstream capital, procurement, relations, and gallery integrations are being reconciled with the new page locations before export.
- Integration coverage caught and fixed a real composition-order defect: staffing domain roots now move into Flock, Operations, Capital, and Records before the legacy Today scroll is adopted, and `RoostStaffingUI` can safely build its interface before `_ready()`. Capital Blueprint, Farmgate, Flock Provisions, applicant, Flock Relations, and Gallery controls now live on their intended pages.
- The review Continue action is page-independent inside Flockwatch, so Gallery filing and skip flows cannot strand the required next step on Today while the player is reading Records. Identity, signals, visibility, deferred focus, and page-scroll preservation are covered.
- Focused authoritative integrations now pass for the compact opening, campus expansion/commute, complete First Clutch induction, staffing/capacity/hiring, Capital Blueprint commissioning, Farmgate routing, Flock Provisions ordering, Flock Relations disposition, and Farmer Relations Gallery credit. The next gate is a fresh browser export and high-resolution visual inspection.

## Durable, truthful Web checkpoints

- Added a deterministic checkpoint coordinator with a 750 ms quiet window, a hard 5-second maximum, success-aware generation tracking, bounded retry scheduling, in-flight mutation safety, and scalar diagnostics. Only routine non-First-Clutch egg production is coalesced; tutorial progress, decisions, review boundaries, staffing, routing, overtime, purchases, contracts, campus actions, and other economic or irreversible mutations still commit immediately.
- Routed browser visibility loss, pagehide, desktop focus loss, application pause, window close, and scene exit through synchronous lifecycle checkpoints. Failed writes remain dirty and the next lifecycle event retries; a monotonic simulation revision prevents same-frame deduplication from dropping a tick that occurred between lifecycle signals.
- Intake remains fail-closed: hiding or exiting an untouched title never creates a default campaign. A stored envelope is now reported only as an unverified Continue candidate until its campaign, simulation, and Senior ledgers activate together; semantically unusable candidates revoke the saved claim, disable Continue, and publish one bounded error. New-file rollback preserves the prior valid campaign.
- Removed the duplicate full-file transaction at First Clutch completion while preserving its immediate `first_clutch_completed` checkpoint. The complete physical Mabel induction, in-flight recovery, presentation, reinvestment, review restoration, and JSON round-trip still pass.
- Replaced unconditional Web autosave copy with evidence-backed states for pending, saving, saved, degraded, best-effort, persistent, and unavailable storage. The wrapper performs a real IndexedDB open/delete probe, combines it with the browser persistence result and Godot `user://` hint, sanitizes errors for assistive narration, and forwards both hidden and pagehide boundaries through a late-bound bridge.
- The final isolated-process Godot 4.7 suite discovers, completes, and passes all 155/155 scripts across three shards with zero failures and zero 300-second watchdog timeouts at `output/godot-full-suite-20260716-durable-checkpoints-final/full-suite-summary.json`. The coordinator and Office integrations cover burst coalescing, maximum/quiet scheduling, retry, in-flight generations, untouched intake, lifecycle tick advancement, semantic-invalid candidates, tutorial immediacy, and truthful diagnostics.
- Node 22 ESLint and the Vinext production build pass; all 26/26 rendered-page tests pass. They cover persistence evidence, persistent/best-effort/unavailable capability classification, runtime-hint precedence, lifecycle forwarding without poisoned deduplication, bounded errors, and accessible status precedence.
- A fresh Chromium persistent profile started a campaign, observed a settled checkpoint, closed the browser, reopened the same profile, presented the stored file only as an unverified candidate, continued it, and restored the exact campaign day/score, Mabel First Clutch state, feed fund, and claim queue. There were zero console, page, stylesheet, script, document, or fetch errors; styled-shell assertions passed before close, at the restart title, and after Continue. Evidence and targeted post-restart captures are in `output/web-game/durable-checkpoint-restart-evidence-final/audit.json`.
- The final responsive audit at 2560x1600, 1440x900, 390x844, and 844x390 reports a loaded exact-16:9 canvas, zero horizontal overflow, and zero console/page errors at `output/web-game/durable-checkpoint-responsive-final/audit.json`; all four captures were inspected. The fresh intake truthfully shows `Checkpoint pending` rather than claiming a save.
- Fresh Godot Web exports are byte-identical across all nine payloads in `docs/` and `web/public/game/`. The final PCK is 5,868,592 bytes with SHA-256 `6ED8A8A099B1AE2144B4470DA5E6D9CCC667C87D62167B7A6F91E2B17F410A48`, and `localhost:3000` serves the exact same bytes and hash.

## Selectable probation filing standards

- Added three explicit, pre-file challenge contracts without changing simulation rules or economy multipliers: Supported Flock (score 45, welfare 45, compliance 55, favor 45, cracks <= 30%), Standard Filing (the shipped 60 / 45 / 55 / 50 / 25%), and Executive Audit (65 / 48 / 65 / 53 / 23%). The title selector discloses every threshold before New Campaign; Continue separately identifies the saved file's immutable terms.
- Campaign schema v2 persists the exact canonical contract ID. Selection is permitted once on a pristine campaign, the public ID is read-only, accepted work and restored files are locked, noncanonical or unknown current-schema IDs are rejected, and schema v1 files migrate only to Standard. Malformed nested preview payloads fail closed as unverified instead of crashing or inventing a label.
- Safeguard forecasts, reports, final outcomes, Flockwatch, tooltips, checkpoints, and Web diagnostics derive from the authoritative contract. Senior Roost suppresses the completed probation contract, and a Supported pass below the old score-only rank band closes as `QUALIFIED ROOSTER` instead of the contradictory `PROBATIONARY MANAGER`.
- Fixed-seed authoritative playthroughs prove all three contracts are achievable and deterministic without ledger drift: welfare-first passes Supported at 54 score / 64 welfare / 73 compliance / 47 favor / 22.09% cracks; Harvest passes Executive at 68 / 50 / 79 / 55 / 22.44%. The Harvest economy ledger is identical under Standard and Executive (156 eggs, $640.91 closing fund).
- Web accessibility distinguishes selected new-file terms from saved Continue terms, republishes selector changes immediately, keeps active and Flockwatch Today narration to pass count plus largest blocker, and reserves all five exact safeguard rows for report/final surfaces. ## Compact opening pod and game-first intake

- Confirmed the opening-density problem in both structure and rendered evidence: a mature-campus frame, disconnected desk ordering, future-capacity signposting, a multi-choice intake, and expanded wrapper reference material all competed before the player had completed one action.
- Reordered the six stable desk IDs onto a connected center/east 2x2 opening pod, with the fifth and sixth saved desk identities revealing the west wing. Simulation IDs, costs, route destinations, save compatibility, and economy receipts remain unchanged. The opening overview is now 17.5 orthographic and grows to 20.75 and 23.5 only when office capacity reaches five and six; collection rails extend from the active desks instead of implying unused floor space.
- Hid the next-perch construction marker throughout Mabel's First Clutch and reveal it only when staffing becomes a live context. Flockwatch now reserves a 438-pixel camera-safe region while preserving the inspected hen and zoom; passive notices respect that focus, while accepted feed, Farmgate, and capacity transactions can deliberately present their physical result and close the drawer.
- Rebuilt campaign intake around one immediate action. A fresh file enters compact setup directly; a valid save presents `Continue Saved File` as the sole primary action and keeps `Review a New File` secondary and non-destructive. ## Camera, runtime-feel, and menu-architecture audit

- Confirmed that bounded panning and zooming fit the management game. The current camera supports contextual wheel zoom only after focusing a hen or landmark; overview wheel input is inert, and keyboard, controller, touch, and trackpad panning are absent. The recommended contract is a locked-isometric camera with clamped free overview, cursor-centered wheel/pinch zoom, mouse/touch drag, keyboard/controller equivalents, an exact Home reset, and no rotation.
- Identified camera correctness and feel gaps independent of runtime cost: static landmark requests above the worker-focus ceiling are silently clamped, touch selection shrinks with the scaled Web canvas, precision-scroll magnitude is ignored, event shots restore the authored overview instead of the prior user view, and one damping rate controls transitions, follow, pan-safe framing, and zoom. Camera computation itself remains constant-time and is not a plausible bottleneck.
- Confirmed structural runtime waste concentrated on the main thread. Accelerated simulation can build and emit a full snapshot repeatedly in one rendered frame; Office then refreshes hidden and visible management surfaces, rebuilds several card/row trees, recomputes presentation state, and serializes an approximately 214 KB, 53-key browser diagnostic on every snapshot. A directional SwiftShader-only sample showed mean animation-frame intervals worsening 53.4% from live 1x to 10x; absolute software-renderer timings are not representative of player hardware, but the relative degradation supports profiling and coalescing accelerated presentation work.
- Confirmed the menu-clutter claim unevenly rather than universally. Intake and compact Today are coherent, while the mature HUD, five-page Flockwatch strip, Operations, Capital, selected-hen dossier, opening policy confirmation, and shift-closing cascade compete for space or duplicate hierarchy. The five-tab Flockwatch row mathematically exceeds its drawer width, the opening policy card can extend far beyond the 720-pixel authored canvas, Capital exposes overlapping facility paths, and Records is comparatively empty.
- Live browser interaction verified New Campaign, Mabel inspection, policy selection and confirmation, routing, Flockwatch, All Filings, page switching, pause and 1x/3x/10x speeds, incident resolution, and contextual wheel zoom without console or page errors. The experience is therefore clunky rather than functionally broken.
- Defined a feature-preserving remediation order: first establish real-hardware frame/allocation/draw-call budgets; then coalesce presentation to once per rendered frame, make domain views revision-driven, and disable or throttle release diagnostics; next correct camera modes and add bounded cross-input pan/zoom; then consolidate HUD and menu hierarchy without removing actions, signals, saves, receipts, or keyboard/controller routes; finally batch Blender/Godot meshes, cache per-chicken node lookups, reduce repeated scene work, and stage checkpoint I/O after instrumentation.
- No production files were changed during this audit. Live evidence is retained in `output/web-game/clunky-analysis/`, including the oversized policy flow, wrapped filings navigation, Operations density, and before/after contextual zoom captures.

## Staged Web startup and truthful loading

- Instrumented Office startup with cumulative milestones and per-facility construction timings. The baseline cold browser path needed 12,195 ms to publish a playable diagnostic even though the 39.5 MB WASM and 5.95 MB PCK finished transferring below 0.7 seconds; synchronous construction of every hidden late-game campus visual accounted for about 4.18 seconds of Office's 6.31-second ready path.
- Split `OfficeStorytelling` into an immediate core office and 18 deferred optional visual steps on graphical builds. The title, intake, camera, core desks, collection chain, and management UI now become interactive first; optional facilities build one per frame, reconcile against the latest authoritative snapshot, preserve presentation visibility and camera footprints, and publish exact completion diagnostics. Headless tests keep deterministic immediate construction.
- Added a focused staged-boot regression proving the core exists before optional roots, all 18 roots finish exactly once, an owned deferred facility becomes visible, and an unowned one stays hidden. The existing comprehensive storytelling test remains unchanged and passing.
- Added release preloads for the Godot JS, manifest, WASM, and PCK, and starts runtime-script and manifest loading in parallel. Resource requests now begin from the initial HTML rather than after hydration. Replaced the misleading saved-record copy with truthful runtime/assets and office-assembly stages plus a semantic progress element.
- Final cold traces reach the playable title in 8,255-8,482 ms on headless SwiftShader versus the 12,195 ms baseline, a 30-32% reduction; the Office scene itself publishes `first_interactive` at 2,722-2,730 ms versus the 6,308 ms synchronous baseline. The exact final audit passes a 10,000 ms cold budget while reporting warm reload separately because full reload includes teardown of the prior 40 MB WebAssembly runtime.
- The four-shard sweep recorded 167 passes and one 120-second resource-contention timeout; the timed-out First Clutch induction scenario passed alone in 113.177 seconds with its full marker. Combined evidence covers all 168/168 Godot scripts with zero assertion failures. The Vinext production build and all 34/34 rendered Web accessibility tests pass.
- The exact final browser client at `output/web-game/boot-final-client/` has no error artifact and reports `loaded=true`, title stage, and 18/18 optional roots complete. The exact final performance evidence is at `output/web-game/boot-performance-final/audit.json`. All nine generated deployables are synchronized between `web/public/game/` and `docs/`; the 5,949,676-byte PCK SHA-256 is `6EB797EFC9700910B675C79AADFFD79FA23874DA0562A33200EDA9154D966D96`.

## Wheeled, readable Feed Party

- Finished the previously documented Feed Party polish gap as one coordinated physical event. The deterministic Blender source now exports a connected mobile trough with four separately named rubber casters, metal brackets and hubs, a push handle, visible feed, sacks, waterer, scoop, signage, and six stable attendance sockets. Source, GLB, and the inspected 1200x900 preview were regenerated together.
- Replaced the floor-rise with a bounded 0.82-second service-lane roll-in and roll-out. All four caster meshes rotate with the cart and reset exactly when hidden; the existing restrained loose-grain burst now fires when the cart actually stops. Reduced-motion mode reveals and retires the same complete event without travel or particle motion.
- Added one pre-synthesized, allocation-free seed-and-beak crunch to the fixed eight-voice audio pool. Each authoritative attendee-ready callback uses a deterministic three-step pitch variation and per-worker semantic limiter, so feeding reads physically without becoming a second reward jingle or growing playback nodes.
- Closed a camera-boundary defect exposed by the first art capture. The compact capacity-four camera correctly hides the dormant west wing, but that also left the Feed Party service bay under the upper-left HUD. Explicit funding now uses the existing reversible event-focus mode, which may frame the authored service bay outside ordinary pan bounds and then restores the player's exact prior pan, zoom, focus, and compact opening frame.
- Closed the remaining visible flock overlap. Blender and runtime attendance sockets are now spaced at local X `-1.55/0/1.55` and Z `+/-1.34`, and the cart moved 35 cm off the contract-board wall. The final native close-up at `captures/feed_party.png` shows four opening hens around the trough without body, wing, face, or accessory overlap; route and physical-staging audits remain clear.
- Added `feed_party_station_test.gd` and `feed_party_event_presentation_test.gd`, and strengthened camera, audio, and layout coverage. The contracts verify four animatable casters, connected handle, six separated sockets, visible feed, zero hidden collisions, roll/reset timing, event framing, physical attendee audio, and the reduced-motion branch.

## Portable career backups

- Closed the remaining production persistence gap for browser players. Settings now includes a compact Career Backup section that exports only a verified campaign checkpoint and restores a portable JSON backup through an explicit replacement confirmation; structural save-shaped files are never presented as verified careers.
- Added bounded, transactional import handling across native and Web builds. The store enforces an 8 MiB input ceiling plus depth, value-count, and string bounds; validates the envelope, schema, migrations, and full composite campaign semantics in disposable state; verifies the temporary write; and preserves the displaced local career as the recovery copy. Rejected files cannot mutate the active campaign or checkpoint.

Web export uses the browser download bridge; native builds use save/open dialogs.

## Reproducible runtime soak and focus-safe camera input

- Added a production-facing engine-health diagnostic inside the existing 4 Hz Web channel: FPS, process/physics cost, Godot static/peak memory where supported, object/node/orphan counts, draw calls, rendered objects, and rendered primitives. The wrapper adds a read-only WebAssembly heap measurement without exposing the engine object. The compact live diagnostic remained below 30 KiB in the final soak.
- Added `npm run audit:soak` and declared Playwright 1.60 as a development dependency so a fresh checkout can reproduce the release gate. The configurable audit uses real user paths for camera pan/zoom, Flockwatch, Settings pause/resume, lifecycle checkpointing, portable JSON download, hidden-picker upload, full semantic replacement, and Continue; each restore must preserve an exact authoritative digest.
- The first soak found that a quick keyboard pan press and release could occur between rendered frames. Camera keys now apply one bounded immediate nudge on the press event while preserving continuous held movement, so a slow frame cannot erase a valid tap.
- The next run found arrow/WASD camera input could depend on which HUD button retained GUI focus. Floor camera keys now run in the pre-GUI input phase; Office disables both pre-GUI and fallback phases whenever a modal or Flockwatch owns input and clears held axes at that boundary. Native coverage proves a focused Flockwatch HUD button cannot swallow the first restored arrow pan or leak movement behind the drawer.
- Camera focus/mode changes now republish through the existing coalescer even when onboarding or another surface pauses authoritative ticks, keeping `render_game_to_text` synchronized with the visible camera. Browser backup selection also returns DOM focus to the canvas after accepted, invalid, oversized, or unavailable-bridge outcomes, preventing a hidden file input from swallowing the next shortcut.
- The final four-cycle SwiftShader stress run passed at `output/web-game/runtime-soak-final2/audit.json`: the 250,085,376-byte WebAssembly heap grew by 0 bytes; post-GC JavaScript grew by 435,040 bytes; Godot settled at +5 objects, +1 node, and 0 orphans; DOM listeners grew by 0; all four 37,071-37,072-byte backups round-tripped exactly; camera feedback arrived in 1,295-1,937 ms under the intentionally slow software renderer; and browser errors/checkpoint failures/audit failures were all zero.
- The final resumed-floor capture at `output/web-game/runtime-soak-final2/final-active-floor.png` and independent client title at `output/web-game/runtime-soak-final-client/shot-0.png` were inspected at original resolution and are clean. The client diagnostic reports the new counters and no error artifact.
- The exact frozen Godot 4.7 aggregate discovers, completes, and passes all 172/172 scripts with zero failures and zero timeouts at `output/godot-full-suite-20260717-runtime-soak-final/full-suite-summary.json`. The Vinext production build and all 35/35 rendered wrapper tests pass.
- All nine release payloads are synchronized between `docs/` and `web/public/game/`, with no unexpected files. Localhost serves the exact 6,089,052-byte PCK with SHA-256 `B18235348827AD738021758720F5B50D9C1BA85FF1D4777F48B7AE78C56A2761`.
- Made the soak reproducible in a fresh checkout by declaring Playwright 1.60.0 and documenting the one-time Chromium install. The Web build toolchain is now Vite 8.1.5, Cloudflare Vite Plugin 1.45.1, and Wrangler 4.112.0. PostCSS is constrained to patched 8.5.14, and only Drizzle Kit's legacy nested loader is constrained to patched esbuild 0.25.12 while Vite/Wrangler retain esbuild 0.28.1. `npm audit` reports 0 production and 0 development vulnerabilities; Node 24 lint, the production build, all 35 wrapper tests, and the Drizzle 0.31.10 CLI all pass.
- The restarted Node 24 localhost server passed a post-upgrade two-cycle audit at `output/web-game/runtime-soak-secure-toolchain/audit.json`: 0-byte WebAssembly growth, slightly lower final post-GC JavaScript use, +5 settled Godot objects, +1 node, 0 orphans, 0 listener growth, and no browser or audit errors. Its inspected final resumed-floor capture remains clean.
- Next evidence-backed quality slice: run the same gate on representative integrated and discrete GPUs plus a touch-first mobile device, then add a longer active-production soak that completes multiple shifts rather than repeatedly restoring one stable live-floor checkpoint.

## Touch-first camera and compact-play remediation

- Confirmed the prior responsive audit proved containment but not phone operability. The focused 844x390 canvas was incorrectly held to 476x268 by a higher-specificity desktop rule, focused play still showed the handbook below the terminal, and touch players had no reliable equivalents for keyboard-only management actions.
- Focused landscape now devotes 530x298 CSS pixels to the authored 16:9 office, hides page-only handbook content, and reduces the terminal header to Page info and Full screen. Portrait focused play hides duplicate Reload/Handbook actions and uses a readable 4+3 touch-control grid instead of squeezing seven labels into one row.
- Added a persistent compact-play dock for Pause, Priority Peck, Zoom +/-, Flockwatch, Overview, and Settings. Every target is at least 40px tall and 86.75px wide in 390x844 portrait (112px wide in 844x390 landscape); the dock remains stable after canvas gestures because visibility is based on focused compact layout rather than mutable pointer-class media queries.
- Added an allow-listed `__pecking_order_mobile_action` Web bridge. It accepts only seven fixed action IDs, exposes no arbitrary method/property/file access, preserves existing modal/input guards, and routes zoom through the camera controller's bounded authority. Touch uses `touchstart` directly; keyboard activation remains available through each semantic button.
- Added `touch-action: none` to the Godot canvas so the browser does not claim floor gestures. One-finger pan remains native device-level touch input; native Godot coverage continues to prove two-finger pinch, while explicit Zoom +/- supplies a deterministic phone fallback.
- Added `web/tests/touch-first-audit.mjs` and `npm run audit:touch`. The release audit at `output/web-game/touch-first-release/audit.json` passes with zero errors/failures, no overflow, hidden handbook, authoritative camera pan `(0.0, 0.82, -3.83)` to `(0.0, 0.65, -3.83)`, zoom `6.3` to `5.625`, and camera input disabled behind both Flockwatch and Settings. Portrait and landscape captures were visually inspected.
- Godot verification: the 172-script aggregate recorded 171 passes, zero assertion failures, and one 90-second harness timeout at `output/godot-full-suite-20260718-touch-first-final/full-suite-summary.json`; the historically long First Clutch induction then passed alone in 126.236 seconds under its correct 210-second ceiling at `output/godot-full-suite-20260718-touch-first-final/first-clutch-rerun/shard-summary.json`. The focused management-camera test also passes.
- Web verification: Vinext production build, ESLint, and all 35/35 rendered wrapper tests pass under Node 24. The required independent Web-game client completed cleanly and its inspected capture is `output/web-game/touch-first-client/shot-0.png`.
- Remaining hardware-only proof is unchanged: test a physical iOS/Android device and representative integrated/discrete GPUs. The locally reproducible touch bridge, compact layouts, authoritative actions, and software-WebGL path now have deterministic coverage.

## Passing Standard career and low-FPS interaction hardening

- Closed the full exported Standard-career proof requested by the prior analysis. `output/web-game/passing-route-v26/probe.json` records all five Farmer reviews, a final score of 71, every probation safeguard passing, entry into Senior Roost, and acceptance of the `standard_board_book` annual mandate. ## Daily policy-to-order strategy briefing

- Shifted from diminishing-return rendering work to the player-facing five-shift loop. The game already had three consequential morning policies and three different scored probation orders per day, but their decision cards did not connect those systems; players had to infer which policy helped or endangered today's goals.
- Added a derived `ORDER FIT / WATCH` briefing to the existing policy cards without adding a modal, currency, reroll, or balance mutation. Selecting a card names the exact supported orders, watched orders, and longer-term output, shell/compliance, or welfare edge; it explicitly says the projection is directional and the closing ledger remains authoritative.
- The mapping follows real simulation modifiers: Harvest supports output/quota/queue goals while watching shell and welfare goals; Assurance supports shell/rework/compliance while watching time-sensitive output; Flock Care supports welfare while watching time-sensitive output. Goals with no direct policy effect, such as farmer favor, are honestly left unclaimed.
- Guidance recomputes from the immutable order catalog on all five probation days and remains visible in the active policy tooltip and machine-readable Web state after authorization. It reads campaign authority only and cannot change score, difficulty, economy, objectives, or save data.
- Replaced clipped full policy names in the three-column cards with their authored short labels (`HARVEST`, `ASSURANCE`, `FLOCK CARE`). Full names, satire, numerical effects, and exact fit remain in the selected preview and diagnostics. This removes a visible truncation defect without increasing menu density.

## Priority Peck precision focus

- Traced the mechanic end to end. Authoritative scoring correctly uses exact claim progress, and the connected chicken flourish already runs on a fixed 0.58-second real-time timeline with ordered three-contact feedback independent of simulation speed.
- A deterministic six-hen probe confirms the opportunity presentation is not speed-equivalent: the standard open window lasts 10.5–20.25 seconds at 1×, 3.5–6.75 seconds at 3×, and only 1.05–2.03 seconds at 10×. The perfect band collapses to 0.15–0.30 seconds at 10× and can survive as only one four-tick-batched Web presentation.
- Implemented the remediation: an inspected hen approaching or inside the Priority Peck window retains the requested speed selection while the effective clock temporarily caps at 1×. The speed control, header guidance, exact dossier result, and Web diagnostic disclose the hold; requested speed restores automatically after the bounded result beat. One intervention per inspection prevents a newly pulled claim from immediately trapping the clock at 1×, while deliberate hen selection rearms the aid.
- Scoring thresholds, rewards, three-charge economy, clean-delivery renewal, authoritative claim progress, and save schema remain unchanged. Web Priority Pecks now enter the existing bounded checkpoint queue so complete career serialization no longer blocks the E-key callback; native and lifecycle checkpoints retain immediate durability.
- The production-flow SwiftShader audit passes at `output/web-game/priority-peck-focus-v9/audit.json`: requested 10× became effective 1× at 17.39% approach progress, the exact claim opened at 31.28%, E settled inside the 2.5-second response gate with a retained `STEADY / +15% / -2.5% shell risk` receipt, and effective 10× restored without immediate re-engagement. ## Authored career case dockets

- The replay audit confirmed that fresh careers reused simulation seed `1701` and a fixed standard-incident formula. Probation therefore repeated the same worker aptitude, claim stream, and incident order; `feed_shortfall` could not appear as an ordinary probation case.
- Added four visible `PO-####` career dockets. `PO-1701` remains the exact shipped/legacy balance schedule. Three replay dockets share the proven `1701` flock and claim economy but own an independent incident RNG and complete four-case bag, preserve the authored onboarding rotation, and prevent adjacent repeats across bag boundaries. Scheduled flock petitions do not consume the bag, and an ineligible petition retains its original structural fallback.
- Advanced `DepartmentSimulation` to strict schema v24 with `career_seed`, Web-safe incident RNG state, remaining bag, and last incident. ## Mastery-aware annual offer rotation and tier-one strategy matrix

- The first portfolio follow-up found a structural replay blocker before target balance: the deterministic three-card scheduler ignored permanent Book mastery. A career could therefore receive a mastered lower-tier repeat in its one variety slot while eligible first-clear Books remained absent.
- New Senior careers now use offer-rotation version 2. Standard remains the guaranteed first no-stake fallback; the hardest unlocked tier remains represented; and the remaining slot prefers an eligible unmastered Book whenever one exists. A focused Year 2 regression reproduces the old `mastered Shell + Executive` catalog and proves the new result is `Mutual + Executive`, both first-clear opportunities. A near-complete tier-three fixture proves Gold remains represented while the final missing Mutual Book receives the variety slot.
- Advanced Senior state to schema v5 with a frozen `offer_rotation_version`. Authentic v1-v4 files migrate to legacy rotation version 1 and retain their exact already-authored cards; new catalogs alone use mastery-aware rotation. Annual-review JSON reconstruction subtracts the just-settled success when rebuilding the opening mastery set, avoiding a second saved portfolio ledger.

It carries a legitimately passed probation simulation through real Senior shifts, incidents, quarterly policies, closing decisions, annual reviews, and Year 2 planning instead of injecting a favorable settlement.

- Kept the frozen Board Books intact and corrected the policy that claimed to support the unreachable care route. Flock Dividend still costs $24, relieves the next quota, adds solidarity/trust, reduces grievance, and costs four farmer favor, but now gives every employed hen +34 morale and removes 34 stress/fatigue. ## Cross-docket Senior career robustness

- Extended the authentic Board Book career lab across all four authored career dockets: `PO-1701`, `PO-4703`, `PO-7919`, and `PO-12011`. Every replay preserves its career seed in the emitted evidence and advances through Shell Stewardship, Mutual Assurance, Rested Flock, and Gold using only public staffing, upgrade, facility, decision, policy, and shift APIs.

The route now reinvests its first Shell year into four keycap tiers, three QA-lamp tiers, two nest-pad tiers, and the Candling Rework Bay; Rested Flock then grows to six hens, finishes all three upgrade tracks, and commissions the Wellness Nest before Gold.
- Locked combined Mutual margins instead of demanding arbitrary overproduction against the adaptive quota. The four dockets now close Mutual at 11/10/10/9 service days, 8.74%/10.89%/12.19%/12.38% cracks, and 98 compliance. Raising keycaps beyond the chosen preparation did not improve the hardest docket's service count because output also raises future quota, so the regression requires the authored 9-day guarantee plus no worse than 14% cracks and at least 90 compliance.

- Fixed the manager-only floating comb without shrinking its rooster silhouette. ## Manager-reference wing restoration

- Identified the real regression using the user's known-good manager chicken. ## Prior-year policy fit bridge

- Carried the annual receipt's binding safeguard into the next year's existing Quarter 1 policy cards. Each of the three irreversible choices now receives one `LAST YEAR` row classifying it as a recovery edge, recovery risk, partial recovery, or no direct edge; passed years use margin-protection language instead of failure language.

## Counterweight case pivots

- Audited all twelve connected-case branches and found that every precedent rewarded repeating the same strategic tone that created it. Care compounded care, executive extraction compounded extraction, and quality compounded quality, producing ideological snowballs instead of meaningful adaptation.
- Re-authored all three incident pairs as counterweights. Each prior response now strengthens the opposite-tone option in its paired case while leaving the original lane available at its normal value. The exact numerical bonuses remain bounded at the same $4, 2-point, or 2-percentage-point scale, but players must decide whether the enhanced pivot is worth changing course.
- Added an explicit `PIVOT OPPORTUNITY` signal to authoritative case memory, the affected response card, every filed future precedent, Flockwatch Today, Web diagnostics, and assistive narration. No menu, currency, save field, or response count was added.

## 2026-07-26 — Content-verified physical acceptance

- Audited the remaining external release path against the original economic-game
  checklist and found a concrete evidence-integrity gap: the physical validator
  checked the shape of a recorded SHA-256 but did not recompute it for a
  repository-relative evidence bundle.
- Repository-relative evidence URIs are now confined to this repository, must
  resolve to an existing file, and must match the recorded SHA-256. Absolute
  local paths, path traversal, missing files, duplicate references, placeholder
  text, malformed hashes, and non-HTTPS external URLs are rejected.
- Fixed one-item JSON array handling so a legitimate single session bundle is
  not unwrapped into a scalar and falsely reported missing. The tester template
  now includes the exact evidence object shape for all seven required sessions.
- Expanded the validator contract from one negative mutation to six independent
  adversarial cases: unsigned decision, mismatched local hash, missing local
  file, repository traversal, software GPU renderer, and failed touch attempt.
  The valid fixture passes and all six invalid fixtures fail for their targeted
  reason.
- The standard beta release gate now runs this contract on every candidate.
  `output/release/physical-contract-full-gate-v1.json` passes all 17 checks:
  11 native release tests, the Node 24 Web toolchain, lint, 42 rendered browser
  tests, production server checks, the physical evidence contract, and exact
  nine-file deploy parity.
- The shipped PCK is unchanged at
  `7BAF2C0C27A0DAE59308BCBF853215C0A6B490FEF64D666C4486287478531553`.
  Physical iOS, Android, desktop/mobile screen-reader, listening, integrated-GPU,
  and discrete-GPU sessions still require human testers and signed evidence;
  this hardening makes those results verifiable but does not claim they occurred.

## 2026-07-26 — Exact-candidate physical handoff

- Upgraded the physical acceptance record to schema v2 after an end-to-end
  protocol audit found that the prior schema could accept the wrong mobile
  browser or reader, incomplete GPU measurement windows, a ratio inconsistent
  with its source medians, or approval recorded before the sessions.
- Touch rows now verify Safari/Chrome platform fit, 44 CSS-pixel targets,
  two-second orientation recovery, and explicit default/1.5x text legibility.
  Reader rows enforce NVDA/VoiceOver on desktop and VoiceOver/TalkBack on mobile.
  Audio rows record the five legacy buses and five semantic cues used by that
  exact-candidate protocol; the current runtime has since expanded to seven
  independently controlled buses.
- GPU rows now declare integrated/discrete class and at least 60 Hz, record the
  60-second warmup, ten-minute sample, both two-minute windows, and their raw
  median FPS values. The validator recomputes the final/initial ratio and rejects
  expanded software, remote, and virtual-renderer signatures.
- UTC chronology is authoritative: release initialization precedes every signed
  session, final approval follows all sessions, and future-dated evidence is
  rejected. Exact commit plus PCK identity remains mandatory; later commits are
  not guessed safe.
- Added `tools/new_physical_release_evidence.ps1`. It refuses dirty tracked
  worktrees, non-HTTPS or placeholder URLs, mismatched shipped PCKs, old
  templates, unsafe output paths, and accidental overwrite, then stamps the
  current full commit/hash/URL/start time into the seven-session record.
- The contract now accepts one valid schema-v2 fixture and rejects 12 independent
  adversarial fixtures one-for-one. The placeholder template parses as seven
  complete rows but intentionally fails 191 pending/placeholder/threshold checks.
- `output/release/physical-schema-v2-full-gate-v1.json` passes all 17 release
  checks, including 11 native tests, Web lint, 42 rendered browser tests,
  production serving, physical-contract self-test, and nine-file deploy parity.
  The seven real human-hardware sessions remain pending.

## 2026-07-28 — Feedback preference controls

- Expanded the independent player-preference contract to schema v5 with three
  release-facing controls: Full/Reduced/Essential Only effect density,
  Brief/Standard/Extended transient-notice duration, and optional haptics on
  supported devices. Schema-v1 through schema-v4 saves and browser mirrors
  migrate to explicit safe defaults.

  its accessible summary. The compact 390x844 and 844x390 layouts remain
  contained; the first long haptics label found by the focused test was shortened
  without removing its complete tooltip or narration.

  while retaining smaller semantic event bursts; Essential Only removes
  decorative particles and lighting pulses. Text, symbols, status labels,
  receipts, warning history, and audio remain complete in every mode.
- Haptics reuse the fixed semantic feedback path instead of creating a second
  event system. Confirmations, denials, alerts, rare outcomes, and milestones
  produce short bounded vibration receipts when enabled; routine production
  noise does not. Disabling haptics leaves the existing visual and audio channels
  intact.
- Focused native verification passes 6/6 for preference persistence/migration,

  audio/haptics, and effect density. The exact settings-copy correction passes
  its 2/2 focused rerun.
